### CVE-2025-61624
- Assigner: fortinet | Published: 2026-04-14 | Updated: 2026-10-17 | Max CVSS: 5.4
- CWE: CWE-22 Escalation of privilege
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 5.4 MEDIUM `CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:H/UI:N/S:U/C:N/I:H/A:H/E:P/RL:O/RC:C`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-16, ransomware=Unknown — Fortinet Multiple Products Path Traversal Vulnerability

An Improper Limitation of a Pathname to a Restricted Directory ('Path Traversal') [CWE-22] vulnerability in Fortinet FortiOS 7.6.0 through 7.6.4, FortiOS 7.4.0 through 7.4.9, FortiOS 7.2 all versions, FortiOS 7.0 all versions, FortiOS 6.4 all versions, FortiPAM 1.7.0, FortiPAM 1.6 all versions, FortiPAM 1.5 all versions, FortiPAM 1.4 all versions, FortiPAM 1.3 all versions, FortiPAM 1.2 all versions, FortiPAM 1.1 all versions, FortiPAM 1.0 all versions, FortiProxy 7.6.0 through 7.6.4, FortiProxy 7.4.0 through 7.4.11, FortiProxy 7.2 all versions, FortiProxy 7.0 all versions, FortiSwitchManager 7.2.0 through 7.2.7, FortiSwitchManager 7.0.0 through 7.0.6 may allow an authenticated attacker with admin profile and at least read-write permissions to write or delete arbitrary files via specific CLI commands.

**solutions:** Upgrade to upcoming  FortiOS version 8.0.0 or above
Upgrade to FortiOS version 7.6.5 or above
Upgrade to FortiOS version 7.4.10 or above
Upgrade to FortiProxy version 7.6.5 or above
Upgrade to FortiProxy version 7.4.12 or above
Upgrade to FortiPAM version 1.8.0 or above
Upgrade to FortiPAM version 1.7.1 or above
Fortinet remediated this issue in FortiSASE version 25.4.b and hence customers do not need to perform any action.
Upgrade to upcoming  FortiSwitch version 8.0.0 or above
Upgrade to FortiSwitch version 7.6.5 or above
Upgrade to FortiSwitch version 7.4.9 or above
Upgrade to FortiSwitchManager version 7.2.8 or above
Upgrade to FortiSwitchManager version 7.0.7 or above

Affected:
- Fortinet / FortiOS: affected 7.6.0 <=7.6.4 (semver); affected 7.4.0 <=7.4.9 (semver); affected 7.2.0 <=7.2.13 (semver); affected 7.0.0 <=7.0.19 (semver); affected 6.4.0 <=6.4.16 (semver)
- Fortinet / FortiProxy: affected 7.6.0 <=7.6.4 (semver); affected 7.4.0 <=7.4.11 (semver); affected 7.2.0 <=7.2.16 (semver); affected 7.0.0 <=7.0.23 (semver)
- Fortinet / FortiSwitchManager: affected 7.2.0 <=7.2.7 (semver); affected 7.0.0 <=7.0.6 (semver)
- Fortinet / FortiPAM: affected 1.7.0; affected 1.6.0 <=1.6.2 (semver); affected 1.5.0 <=1.5.1 (semver); affected 1.4.0 <=1.4.3 (semver); affected 1.3.0 <=1.3.1 (semver); affected 1.2.0; affected 1.1.0 <=1.1.2 (semver); affected 1.0.0 <=1.0.3 (semver)
- Siemens / RUGGEDCOM APE1808: affected 0 <* (custom)

References:
- https://fortiguard.fortinet.com/psirt/FG-IR-26-122
- https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/fortimanager-cve-2024-47575-peerstitch-malware/#post-188095-_guyq53ar8k05 [third-party-advisory]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2025-61624 [government-resource]
- https://cert-portal.siemens.com/productcert/html/ssa-975644.html

KEV short description: FortiOS, FortiPAM, FortiProxy, and FortiSwitchManager contain a path traversal vulnerability that may allow an authenticated attacker with admin profile and at least read-write permissions to write or delete arbitrary files via specific CLI commands.

- CWE: CWE-918 CWE-918 Server-Side Request Forgery (SSRF)
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 4.3 MEDIUM `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N`

- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-20, ransomware=Unknown — Plex Media Server Server-Side Request Forgery (SSRF) Vulnerability

### CVE-2026-96656 — Plex Media Server arbitrary file write
- Assigner: cisa-cg | Published: 2026-09-23 | Updated: 2026-10-21 | Max CVSS: 8.6
- CWE: CWE-73 CWE-73 External Control of File Name or Path
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 8.6 HIGH `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 7.2 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:H/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (cna): Exploitation=none, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-20, ransomware=Unknown — Plex Media Server External Control of File Name or Path Vulnerability

Plex Media Server before 1.43.3.10861 allows an admin user to write arbitrary files that may be executed on load. The preference TranscoderH264Options is appended verbatim to x264's option string on every transcode. At startup, all .so files are run without signature, execute bit, or symbol checks.

Affected:
- Plex / Media Server: affected 0 <1.43.3.10861 (custom); unaffected 1.43.3.10861

Credits: Zach Main

References:

### CVE-2026-110936
- Assigner: siemens | Published: 2026-10-15 | Updated: 2026-10-21 | Max CVSS: 8.4
- CWE: CWE-427 CWE-427: Uncontrolled Search Path Element
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 6.7 MEDIUM `CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:H/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 8.4 HIGH `CVSS:4.0/AV:L/AC:L/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-20, ransomware=Unknown — Siemens Software Center Uncontrolled Search Path Element Vulnerability

A vulnerability has been identified in Siemens Software Center (All versions < V4.0.9.8). The affected application loads dynamic-link libraries (DLLs) from an uncontrolled search path. This could allow an authenticated local attacker to place a specially crafted DLL in a directory that the application searches, leading to arbitrary code execution with the privileges of the affected application.

Affected:
- Siemens / Siemens Software Center: affected 0 <V4.0.9.8 (custom)

### CVE-2026-114625 — Security Advisory 0199
- Assigner: Arista | Published: 2026-10-22 | Updated: 2026-10-24 | Max CVSS: 9.9
- CWE: CWE-89 CWE-89 Improper Neutralization of Special Elements used in an SQL Command ('SQL Injection')
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 9.9 CRITICAL `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 9.4 CRITICAL `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:N/PR:L/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:H/SI:H/SA:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-23, ransomware=Unknown — Arista VeloCloud Orchestrator SQL Injection Vulnerability

VeloCloud Orchestrator (VCO) on-prem has a security issue where this issue may allow a remote attacker to access privileged internal functionality and impact the VCO host. Successful exploitation may compromise the confidentiality, integrity, and availability of the orchestrator and data managed by the orchestrator.

On an affected system, any authenticated user can gain operator privileges and masquerade as a local "superuser operator" user. This includes access to functionality that is intended for internal use only and which is not intended to be remotely accessible.

Hosted, including Dedicated, versions of VCO were impacted and have already been patched.

**workarounds:** Until the fixed software is deployed, operators should apply defense-in-depth controls appropriate for their environment:

- Restrict access to the VCO web interface to trusted administrative networks.
- Monitor the VCO for accesses from known malicious source IPs.
- Monitor for unexpected outbound network activity from the VCO host.
- Consider blocking outbound ports not needed for normal activities.
- Monitor for backdoor daemons and webshells.
- Review recent administrator activity for unexpected changes.

**solutions:** The recommended resolution is to upgrade to a fixed VCO release at your earliest convenience.

These vulnerabilities have been fixed in the following releases:

- VCO 5.2.3.18 and later in the 5.2 train
- VCO 6.1.3.10 and later in the 6.1 train
- VCO 6.4.2.12 and later in the 6.4 train

Affected:
- Arista Networks / VeloCloud Orchestrator: affected 5.2.0 <5.2.3.18 (custom); affected 6.1.0 <6.1.3.10 (custom); affected 6.4.0 <6.4.2.12 (custom)

References:
- https://www.arista.com/en/support/advisories-notices/security-advisory/24838-security-advisory-0199 [vendor-advisory]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-114625 [government-resource]

KEV short description: Arista VeloCloud Orchestrator on-prem contains a SQL injection vulnerability that may allow a remote attacker to access privileged internal functionality and impact the VCO host. Successful exploitation may compromise the confidentiality, integrity, and availability of the orchestrator and data managed by the orchestrator.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). ### CVE-2026-109618 — RCE via OOM corruption
- Assigner: php | Published: 2026-10-21 | Updated: 2026-11-05 | Max CVSS: 9.2
- CWE: CWE-416 CWE-416 Use after free; CWE-755 CWE-755 Improper Handling of Exceptional Conditions; CWE-824 CWE-824 Access of uninitialized pointer; CWE-1325 CWE-1325 Improperly controlled sequential memory allocation; CWE-1419 CWE-1419 Incorrect Initialization of Resource
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 9.2 CRITICAL `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:H/AT:P/PR:N/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-26, ransomware=Unknown — PHP Memory Corruption Vulnerability
- Exploit-DB 52704: PHP2Shell (CVE-2026-109618) - PHP core versions after v5.4 - RCE (remote/php, 2026-10-22, verified=1)

PHP uses longjmp() for out-of-memory (OOM) errors to exit the current context and abort handling of the request. Some contexts may not properly account for this scenario and leave request memory in an uninitialized state before performing an allocation. The subsequent request cleanup procedure may read or branch on this memory. Multiple OOM strategies can consistently trigger this condition and lead to remote code execution (RCE).

**workarounds:** Check the GitHub Security Advisory for mitigation strategies.

Affected:
- PHP Group / PHP: affected 8.2.* <8.2.35 (semver); affected 8.3.* <8.3.36 (semver); affected 8.4.* <8.4.27 (semver); affected 8.5.* <8.5.12 (semver)

### CVE-2026-109619 — RCE via stale global state
- Assigner: php | Published: 2026-10-21 | Updated: 2026-11-05 | Max CVSS: 9.2
- CWE: CWE-416 CWE-416 Use after free; CWE-755 CWE-755 Improper Handling of Exceptional Conditions; CWE-824 CWE-824 Access of uninitialized pointer; CWE-1325 CWE-1325 Improperly controlled sequential memory allocation; CWE-1419 CWE-1419 Incorrect Initialization of Resource
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 9.2 CRITICAL `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:H/AT:P/PR:N/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-26, ransomware=Unknown — PHP Stale Internal Request-State Vulnerability

PHP has various global state that temporarily points into request memory. In some scenarios, global state may not be correctly reset, or may be repopulated after being reset. The global state may then refer to request memory even after it has been freed. Subsequent requests may read or branch on this stale state before it is fully initialized. Multiple strategies can trigger this condition and produce remote code execution (RCE).

**workarounds:** Check the GitHub Security Advisory for mitigation strategies.

Affected:
- PHP Group / PHP: affected 8.2.* <8.2.35 (semver); affected 8.3.* <8.3.36 (semver); affected 8.4.* <8.4.27 (semver); affected 8.5.* <8.5.12 (semver)

### CVE-2026-111497 — Out-of-Bounds Write in Google Brotli Decoder
- Assigner: Google | Published: 2026-10-22 | Updated: 2026-11-10 | Max CVSS: 9.2
- CWE: CWE-787 CWE-787 Out-of-bounds Write; CWE-252 CWE-252 Unchecked Return Value
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 9.2 CRITICAL `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:P/PR:N/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-27, ransomware=Unknown — Google Brotli Unchecked Return Value Vulnerability

A buffer overflow vulnerability in the Brotli decoder (c/dec/decode.c) in Google Brotli versions 1.0.3 through 1.2.0 on all platforms allows a remote unauthenticated attacker to corrupt adjacent decoder state memory, disclose sensitive memory contents, cause a denial of service, or potentially execute arbitrary code using a crafted Brotli-compressed stream.





Users need to update any Brotli libraries or binaries between 1.0.3 through 1.2.0 to the latest available version: 1.0.10 or 1.1.1 or 1.2.1.

**solutions:** Update any Brotli libraries or binaries between 1.0.3 through 1.2.0 to the latest available version: 1.0.10 or 1.1.1 or 1.2.1.

Affected:

### CVE-2026-46300 — net: skbuff: preserve shared-frag marker during coalescing
- Assigner: Linux | Published: 2026-05-23 | Updated: 2026-10-29 | Max CVSS: 7.8
- CWE: CWE-123 Write-what-where Condition; CWE-456 CWE-456 Missing Initialization of a Variable
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 7.8 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- CVSS 3.1 (redhat-SADP): 7.8 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-28, ransomware=Unknown — Linux Kernel Missing Initialization of a Variable Vulnerability
- Exploit-DB 52591: Linux Kernel -  Local Privilege Escalation (local/linux, 2026-05-29, verified=0)

In the Linux kernel, the following vulnerability has been resolved:

net: skbuff: preserve shared-frag marker during coalescing

skb_try_coalesce() can attach paged frags from @from to @to.  If @from
has SKBFL_SHARED_FRAG set, the resulting @to skb can contain the same
externally-owned or page-cache-backed frags, but the shared-frag marker
is currently lost.

That breaks the invariant relied on by later in-place writers.  In
particular, ESP input checks skb_has_shared_frag() before deciding
whether an uncloned nonlinear skb can skip skb_cow_data().  If TCP
receive coalescing has moved shared frags into an unmarked skb, ESP can
see skb_has_shared_frag() as false and decrypt in place over page-cache
backed frags.

Propagate SKBFL_SHARED_FRAG when skb_try_coalesce() transfers paged
frags.  The tailroom copy path does not need the marker because it copies
bytes into @to's linear data rather than transferring frag descriptors.

Affected:
- Linux / Linux: affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <3599e6b3cc1ada96883d496a50a210d3afbb6987 (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <2f2b16022a2e10ca7bccfb98db5ed2ec0f72641c (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <9d3e5fd19fe1063bf607219e8562fbd567b8e8d5 (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <78bf6b6bb19541d19fbda6242e7cfe2c682763c0 (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <760e1addc27ba1a7beb4a0a7e8b3e9ec49e7a34e (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <3bd9e113d50034db99d7ef69fd8e5242d15e414a (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <3884358a9286b17f389a72b1426fc4547c23c111 (git); affected cef401de7be8c4e155c6746bfccf721a4fa5fab9 <f84eca5817390257cef78013d0112481c503b4a3 (git) files: net/core/skbuff.c

KEV short description: Linux Kernel contains a missing initialization of a variable vulnerability that could allow for unintended modification of page-cache-backed data via failure to preserve the shared-fragment marker during socket buffer coalescing, causing ESP input processing to decrypt shared fragments in place. This vulnerability can be chained with CVE-2026-109618 and CVE-2026-109619 to implant PHP webshells and run commands as root.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). Follow applicable BOD 26-04 guidance for cloud services or discontinue use of the product if mitigations are unavailable. Stakeholders are responsible for evaluating each asset's internet exposure and ensuring adherence to BOD 26-04 patching guidelines.
Notes: This vulnerability affects an open-source component, third-party library, protocol, or proprietary implementation that could be used by different products. For more information, please see: ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/3599e6b3cc1ada96883d496a50a210d3afbb6987 ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/2f2b16022a2e10ca7bccfb98db5ed2ec0f72641c ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/9d3e5fd19fe1063bf607219e8562fbd567b8e8d5 ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/78bf6b6bb19541d19fbda6242e7cfe2c682763c0 ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/760e1addc27ba1a7beb4a0a7e8b3e9ec49e7a34e ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/3bd9e113d50034db99d7ef69fd8e5242d15e414a ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/3884358a9286b17f389a72b1426fc4547c23c111 ; https://git.kernel.org/stable/c/f84eca5817390257cef78013d0112481c503b4a3 ; BOD 26-04: https://www.cisa.gov/news-events/directives/bod-26-04-prioritizing-security-updates-based-risk ; Forensics Triage Requirements: https://www.cisa.gov/news-events/directives/bod-26-04-implementation-guidance-prioritizing-security-updates-based-risk ; https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-46300

### CVE-2026-97229 — Integer Overflow or Wraparound in GitLab
- Assigner: GitLab | Published: 2026-10-13 | Updated: 2026-10-30 | Max CVSS: 10
- CWE: CWE-190 CWE-190: Integer Overflow or Wraparound
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 10 CRITICAL `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-29, ransomware=Unknown — GitLab Community Edition and Enterprise Edition Integer Overflow or Wraparound Vulnerability

GitLab has remediated an issue in GitLab CE/EE affecting all versions from 19.2 before 19.2.8, 19.3 before 19.3.5, and 19.4 before 19.4.2 that, under certain conditions, could have allowed an unauthenticated user to execute arbitrary code on the GitLab server via a specially crafted regular expression in a CI/CD configuration, due to insufficient regular expression validation.

**solutions:** Upgrade to versions 19.2.8, 19.3.5, 19.4.2 or above.

Affected:
- GitLab / GitLab: affected 19.2 <19.2.8 (semver); affected 19.3 <19.3.5 (semver); affected 19.4 <19.4.2 (semver)

Credits: Thanks [joaxcar](https://hackerone.com/joaxcar) for reporting this vulnerability through our HackerOne bug bounty program

KEV short description: GitLab Community Edition and Enterprise Edition contain a integer overflow or wraparound vulnerability that could allow an unauthenticated user to execute arbitrary code on the GitLab server via a specially crafted regular expression in a CI/CD configuration, due to insufficient regular expression validation.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). Follow applicable BOD 26-04 guidance for cloud services or discontinue use of the product if mitigations are unavailable. Stakeholders are responsible for evaluating each asset's internet exposure and ensuring adherence to BOD 26-04 patching guidelines.
Notes: Other products can potentially be affected by the same underlying Ruby/Onigmo Integer Overflow issue in CI/CD Regular Expression Compiler. ### CVE-2026-76418 — Cisco Secure Firewall Adaptive Security Appliance and Secure Firewall Threat Defense Software Remote Access SSL VPN Tunnel Denial of Service Vulnerability
- Assigner: cisco | Published: 2026-10-28 | Updated: 2026-10-30 | Max CVSS: 8.6
- CWE: CWE-690 Unchecked Return Value to NULL Pointer Dereference
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 8.6 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:C/C:N/I:N/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=partial
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-10-29, ransomware=Unknown — Cisco Secure Firewall ASA Software and Cisco Secure FTD Software Unchecked Return Value to NULL Pointer Dereference Vulnerability

A vulnerability in the Remote Access SSL VPN service of Cisco Secure Firewall Adaptive Security Appliance (ASA) Software and Cisco Secure Firewall Threat Defense (FTD) Software could allow an unauthenticated, remote attacker to cause the device to reload unexpectedly, resulting in a denial of service (DoS) condition. This vulnerability is due to logic errors when secondary authentication is configured on a tunnel group. An attacker could exploit this vulnerability by sending a crafted request when connecting to the SSL remote access VPN. A successful exploit could allow the attacker to cause the affected device to reload, resulting in a DoS condition.

**exploits:** In October 2026, the Cisco PSIRT became aware of active exploitation of this vulnerability. Cisco strongly recommends that customers upgrade to a fixed software release to remediate this vulnerability.

### CVE-2026-119977 — WordPress Core 4.7.0 - 7.1.3 - Unauthenticated Upgrade-Window Staged-Installer Remote Code Execution
- Assigner: Wordfence | Published: 2026-10-31 | Updated: 2026-11-04 | Max CVSS: 8.1

### CVE-2026-102895 — GPUStack: Authentication bypass via trusted loopback source (reverse-proxy TCP peer or X-GPUStack-Real-IP header) grants unauthenticated admin
- Assigner: GitHub_M | Published: 2026-11-04 | Updated: 2026-11-10 | Max CVSS: 9.8
- CWE: CWE-287 CWE-287: Improper Authentication; CWE-290 CWE-290: Authentication Bypass by Spoofing; CWE-346 CWE-346: Origin Validation Error
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 9.8 CRITICAL `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-09, ransomware=Unknown — GPUStack Improper Authentication Vulnerability

GPUStack is an open-source GPU cluster manager for AI model serving and GPU instance provisioning. Prior to 2.2.0, get_current_user in gpustack/api/auth.py treated a loopback request source as the built-in administrator when force_auth_localhost was false, which was the default. A remote request could appear to originate from 127.0.0.1 because client_ip_getter accepted a same-host reverse proxy peer or a client-supplied X-GPUStack-Real-IP loopback value. The bypass allowed unauthenticated administrative access, including user and model changes, persistent administrator API-key creation, disclosure of worker and cloud-provider secrets, and code execution through POST /v2/inference-backends/from-yaml. Exploitation was observed in the wild against a 2.1.1 deployment. This issue is fixed in version 2.2.0.

Affected:
- gpustack / gpustack: affected < 2.2.0

References:
- https://github.com/gpustack/gpustack/security/advisories/GHSA-gj9m-f8ph-pmhr [x_refsource_CONFIRM]
- https://github.com/gpustack/gpustack/pull/5346 [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://github.com/gpustack/gpustack/commit/53f42a6a553542974396d16a4ff6b12bcbedb48d [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://github.com/gpustack/gpustack/releases/tag/v2.2.0 [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-102895 [government-resource]

KEV short description: GPUStack contains an improper authentication vulnerability which could allow unauthenticated administrative access, including user and model changes, persistent administrator API-key creation, disclosure of worker and cloud-provider secrets, and code execution through POST /v2/inference-backends/from-yaml.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). Follow applicable BOD 26-04 guidance for cloud services or discontinue use of the product if mitigations are unavailable. ### CVE-2026-116779 — FreeSWITCH: Inbound SIP MESSAGE can select the api chat proto and run FreeSWITCH API commands
- Assigner: GitHub_M | Published: 2026-11-05 | Updated: 2026-11-10 | Max CVSS: 9.8
- CWE: CWE-77 CWE-77: Improper Neutralization of Special Elements used in a Command ('Command Injection'); CWE-78 CWE-78: Improper Neutralization of Special Elements used in an OS Command ('OS Command Injection'); CWE-749 CWE-749: Exposed Dangerous Method or Function
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 9.8 CRITICAL `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-09, ransomware=Unknown — SignalWire FreeSwitch Code Injection Vulnerability

FreeSWITCH is a Software Defined Telecom Stack enabling the digital transformation from proprietary telecom switches to a software implementation that runs on any commodity hardware. Prior to 1.11.3, sofia_presence_handle_sip_i_message() split the To user of an inbound SIP MESSAGE on a plus sign and treated the left side as a chat protocol selector. Selecting the api chat proto reached switch_api_execute(), allowing a peer whose MESSAGE was accepted to run FreeSWITCH API commands, including operating-system command sinks, with the daemon's privileges. Inbound chat is enabled on the stock internal and external profiles, and any authenticated profile account is sufficient; stock directory users without password material can also remove that barrier in affected configurations. The flaw permits configuration and credential disclosure, arbitrary call control, and command execution, and exploitation has been observed in the wild. This issue is fixed in version 1.11.3.

Affected:
- signalwire / freeswitch: affected < 1.11.3

References:
- https://github.com/signalwire/freeswitch/security/advisories/GHSA-mg63-h38w-8p9p [x_refsource_CONFIRM]
- https://github.com/signalwire/freeswitch/pull/3135 [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://github.com/signalwire/freeswitch/commit/7015f6a73ab3d67fed5fbfc277ecfb88a74c2a86 [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://github.com/signalwire/freeswitch/releases/tag/v1.11.3 [x_refsource_MISC]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-116779 [government-resource]

### CVE-2026-123158
- Assigner: mitre | Published: 2026-11-05 | Updated: 2026-11-10 | Max CVSS: 8.1
- CWE: CWE-394 CWE-394 Unexpected Status Code or Return Value
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 8.1 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:H/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-09, ransomware=Unknown — OpenBSD OpenSSH Unexpected Status Code or Return Value Vulnerability

sshd in OpenSSH before 10.6p2 on Linux, when musl libc is used without PAM, lacks a workaround for a password authentication bypass (related to misinterpretation of whether a string is intended to be a password hash). For some configurations of accounts, any password between 256 and 1024 characters succeeds. An example of a platform using musl libc without PAM is Alpine Linux.

Affected:
- OpenBSD / OpenSSH: affected 0 <10.6p2 (custom)

References:
- https://www.openssh.org/releasenotes.html#10.6p2
- https://github.com/openssh/openssh-portable/commit/4353fdc43ef646c43a8dda7025cc1a898caf1482

### CVE-2026-123218 — IBM Aspera Faspex Authorization Bypass

Please follow the instructions  here https://www.ibm.com/docs/en/aspera-faspex/5.0.x .



Validate that the faspex-core container image is 5.0.19 (1ce4b13, Fix Pack: 1)

Affected:
- IBM / Aspera Faspex: affected 5.0.0 <=5.0.19 (semver)

### CVE-2026-20320
- Assigner: cisco | Published: 2026-08-19 | Updated: 2026-11-11 | Max CVSS: 7.5
- CWE: CWE-611 Improper Restriction of XML External Entity Reference
- CVSS 3.1 (cna): 7.5 HIGH `CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:N/A:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=partial
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-10, ransomware=Unknown — Cisco BroadWorks Improper Restriction of XML External Entity Reference Vulnerability

A vulnerability in the Open Client Interface (OCI) XML Parser of Cisco BroadWorks could allow an unauthenticated, remote attacker to read sensitive configuration information on an affected system.
 This vulnerability exists because XML entries are improperly parsed due to external entity resolution being allowed by default. An attacker could exploit this vulnerability by sending a crafted XML message to the Open Client Interface &ndash; Provisioning (OCI-P) service. A successful exploit could allow the attacker to view sensitive files from the filesystem with the privileges of the Cisco BroadWorks user.

**exploits:** The Cisco PSIRT is not aware of any public announcements or malicious use of the vulnerability that is described in this advisory.

Affected:
- Cisco / Cisco BroadWorks: affected N/A

References:
- https://sec.cloudapps.cisco.com/security/center/content/CiscoSecurityAdvisory/cisco-sa-bworks-xxe-uwUd7CEt
- https://malbearlabs.com/posts/operation-snowball-inside-an-autonomous-ai-intrusion-campaign [third-party-advisory]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-20320 [government-resource]

KEV short description: Cisco BroadWorks contains an improper restriction of XML external entity reference vulnerability that could allow an unauthenticated, remote attacker to read sensitive configuration information on an affected system.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). Follow applicable BOD 26-04 guidance for cloud services or discontinue use of the product if mitigations are unavailable. Stakeholders are responsible for evaluating each asset's internet exposure and ensuring adherence to BOD 26-04 patching guidelines.

### CVE-2026-111560 — OS Command Injection in Barracuda Appliances
- Assigner: Mandiant | Published: 2026-11-02 | Updated: 2026-11-11 | Max CVSS: 8.6
- CWE: CWE-78 CWE-78 Improper neutralization of special elements used in an OS command ('OS command injection')
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 8.6 HIGH `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=no, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-10, ransomware=Unknown — Barracuda Networks Multiple Products OS Command Injection Vulnerability

Improper neutralization of shell metacharacters in the timezone configuration field in certain Barracuda appliance products using common Barracuda code allows an authenticated administrator to inject OS commands that execute with root privileges when the crafted timezone value is subsequently processed.

Affected:
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Email Security Gateway (ESG): affected 0 <=9.4.0.27 (custom)
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Load Balancer Application Delivery Controller (ADC): affected 0 <=6.4.0 (custom)
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Web Application Firewall (WAF): affected 0 <=11.0.1 (custom)
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Web Security Gateway (WSG): affected 0 <=16.1.100.100 (custom)
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Message Archiver (BMA): affected 0 <=5.5.4.0 (custom)
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda WAF Control Center (WCC): affected 0 <=2.2.1 (custom)

References:
- https://github.com/mandiant/Vulnerability-Disclosures/blob/master/2026/MNDT-2026-0041.md [third-party-advisory]
- https://trust.barracuda.com/security/pre-authentication-authorization-bypass-command-injection [vendor-advisory]
- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-111560 [government-resource]

KEV short description: Barracuda Networks Email Security Gateway (ESG), Load Balancer, Application Delivery Controller (ADC), Message Archiver (BMA), WAF Control Center (WCC), Web Application Firewall (WAF), and Web Security Gateway (WSG) contain an OS command injection vulnerability. This vulnerability could allow an authenticated administrator to inject OS commands that execute with root privileges when the crafted timezone value is subsequently processed. This vulnerability can be chained with CVE-2026-116606
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes). Follow applicable BOD 26-04 guidance for cloud services or discontinue use of the product if mitigations are unavailable. Stakeholders are responsible for evaluating each asset's internet exposure and ensuring adherence to BOD 26-04 patching guidelines.
Notes: https://trust.barracuda.com/security/pre-authentication-authorization-bypass-command-injection ; https://github.com/mandiant/Vulnerability-Disclosures/blob/master/2026/MNDT-2026-0041.md ; BOD 26-04: https://www.cisa.gov/news-events/directives/bod-26-04-prioritizing-security-updates-based-risk ; Forensics Triage Requirements: https://www.cisa.gov/news-events/directives/bod-26-04-implementation-guidance-prioritizing-security-updates-based-risk ; https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-111560

### CVE-2026-116606 — Authentication Bypass Allows Global Configuration Restore in Barracuda Email Security Gateway
- Assigner: Mandiant | Published: 2026-11-02 | Updated: 2026-11-11 | Max CVSS: 9.3
- CWE: CWE-288 CWE-288 Authentication bypass using an alternate path or channel
- CVSS 4.0 (cna): 9.3 CRITICAL `CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:N/PR:N/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N`
- SSVC (CISA-ADP): Exploitation=active, Automatable=yes, Technical Impact=total
- **CISA KEV**: added 2026-11-10, ransomware=Unknown — Barracuda Networks Email Security Gateway (ESG) Authentication Bypass Using an Alternate Path or Channel Vulnerability

An authentication bypass in Barracuda Email Security Gateway versions earlier than 9.4.0.030 allows an unauthenticated remote attacker to invoke a configuration-import function and restore a crafted, compatible backup archive, resulting in unauthorized modification of the appliance’s global configuration.

Affected:
- Barracuda Networks / Barracuda Email Security Gateway (ESG): affected 0 <9.4.0.030 (custom)

References:
- https://github.com/mandiant/Vulnerability-Disclosures/blob/master/2026/MNDT-2026-0042.md [third-party-advisory]

- https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog?field_cve=CVE-2026-116606 [government-resource]

KEV short description: Barracuda Networks Email Security Gateway (ESG) contains an authentication bypass using an alternate path or channel that could allow an unauthenticated remote attacker to invoke a configuration-import function and restore a crafted, compatible backup archive, resulting in unauthorized modification of the appliance’s global configuration. This vulnerability can be chained with CVE-2026-111560.
Required action: Apply mitigations in accordance with vendor instructions, ensuring compliance with CISA’s BOD 26-04 Prioritizing Security Updates Based on Risk (see URL in Notes) guidance and CISA’s “Forensics Triage Requirements” (see URL in Notes).
